Step.lean只有定义
/-
# Step —— 一步怎么走：**只做分发，0 条定理**

每一支都交给一条规则（`Rule/`），这里不加任何新说法：

    方向键            工位 := Turn.turnBy
    倾倒              容器 := Pour.pour
    点击              点中色环 → 颜料色 := PickPaint；点中在场小杯描边 → 容器 := Stamp.stamp；否则原样
    重来              局面 := BackToStart.start

算完这一步之后（重来除外）按顺序问两件事：

    容器 = 目标图？（LevelUp.isGoal）     → `won`
    钟走到这一步预算归零？（GameOver.overAt） → `over`
    都不是                                 → `ok`，钟 +1

「过关与归零同时发生时谁先」语料里没有这样一步；这里取过关在先，**这一句没被检验过**。
-/

import Rule.Move.Turn
import Rule.Move.PickPaint
import Rule.Move.SpoutFollows
import Rule.Move.Pour
import Rule.Move.Stamp
import Rule.Move.Budget
import Rule.Outcome.LevelUp
import Rule.Outcome.GameOver
import Rule.Respond.BackToStart

namespace Game

/-- 这一步之后的状态（钟不动）。 -/
def act (b : Board) (s : St) : Act → St
  | .click r c =>
      match swatchHit b.palette r c with
      | some _ => { s with paint := pickPaint b s.paint r c }
      | none   => if stampHit b s r c then { s with vessel := stamp s } else s
  | .pour  => { s with vessel := pour s }
  | .reset => start
  | a      => { s with pose := turnBy s.pose a }

def step (b : Board) (s : St) : Act → Res
  | .reset => .ok start
  | a =>
    if isGoal b (act b s a) then .won
    else if overAt (s.clock + 1) then .over
    else .ok { act b s a with clock := s.clock + 1 }

end Game
封存时的单文件形式化（修复后）

封存这一局时一并写下的单文件 Lean：把学到的规则写成 stepRules，证明两件事——consistent（每条规则在全部录像转移上成立）与 faithful（照规则从第 1 关一步步算，整局走到 WIN 且与录像逐步一致）。2026-09-28 起按冻结判据逐局修过：证明一律由内核 decide 检查（不用 native_decide），关卡内每一步由规则算出、只有换关查表，路线是从第 1 关起的整局；每局另经独立审计。审计查出的局限照实写在文件头注的「公示」里。

cd82-20260912/formal/Game.lean
检查器全绿规则 30 条路线 70 步，换关查表 6 步公理：propext
-- REGENERATED: lib=meta/repair-20260928/lib/cd82-20260912/_lib.py ; why=原状态把色板压成计数、预算钟无读数，补 ring_code（色环位置与芯色）、panel_cells（面板逐格颜色）、vessel_cells（容器逐格颜色）与隐藏状态 h_k（自最近归零以来的步数），路线取整局（第 0 关到 WIN）
-- 由 world/formalize.py 生成于 2026-09-29T14:26:05+08:00；局 cd82；基于 run ['cd82-0905-1311']
-- 这个文件证明的是「规则解释全部观测」与「规则下路线必胜」，证明不了「规则 = 真实游戏」。
-- 数据段与定理段由脚本生成，重跑 build 会覆盖；规则段是借来的眼睛写的，脚本不碰。

-- （数据段 748 行从略：由 world/formalize.py 从原始录像转移生成）

-- ==== 规则段 BEGIN（借来的眼睛写；脚本不碰这一段）====
-- 修复版规则段（meta/repair-20260928）。原则：
--   * 关卡内每一步由 stepRules 从当前状态真算出来：姿势、第二只小杯、倾倒、盖章、选色、下划线、预算钟。
--   * 会不会过关由 levelup_needs_target_pic 判定；过关后的新关（面板、色板、目标图）是游戏内容，查 worldRows。
--   * 位掩码一律转成 Nat 用 ||| &&& ^^^ <<< >>> 做（内核有 GMP 加速），不逐格扫 4096 位。
--   * 31 条概念里写进 30 条；budget_zero_gameover 没写，理由见 rules 列表前的注释。

-- ---------------- 动作与方向（_lib.KEY_DIR / turn / DIR_VEC） ----------------
def isA5 (a : Act) : Bool := match a with | Act.a5 => true | _ => false
def isA6 (a : Act) : Bool := match a with | Act.a6 _ _ => true | _ => false
def isReset (a : Act) : Bool := match a with | Act.reset => true | _ => false

-- 四个键 = 屏幕四方向：A1 北(2) / A2 南(6) / A3 西(4) / A4 东(0)
def keyDir (a : Act) : Option Int :=
  match a with
  | Act.a1 => some 2
  | Act.a2 => some 6
  | Act.a3 => some 4
  | Act.a4 => some 0
  | _ => none

def isKey (a : Act) : Bool := (keyDir a).isSome

-- ACTION6:x,y -> (行 y, 列 x)
def clickOf (a : Act) : Option (Int × Int) :=
  match a with
  | Act.a6 x y => some (Int.ofNat y, Int.ofNat x)
  | _ => none

def offset8 (a b : Int) : Int := (a - b + 4) % 8 - 4

-- _lib.turn：开口转离键方向 45 度；夹角 45/90 度才转得动，否则 none（死键）
def turn8 (d k : Int) : Option Int :=
  let o := offset8 d k
  if o == 1 || o == 2 then some ((d + 1) % 8)
  else if o == -1 || o == -2 then some ((d + 7) % 8)
  else none

def turnOf (s : State) (a : Act) : Option Int :=
  match keyDir a with
  | some k => turn8 s.open_dir k
  | none => none

def dirVec (d : Int) : Int × Int :=
  if d == 0 then (0, 1) else if d == 1 then (-1, 1) else if d == 2 then (-1, 0)
  else if d == 3 then (-1, -1) else if d == 4 then (0, -1) else if d == 5 then (1, -1)
  else if d == 6 then (1, 0) else (1, 1)

-- _lib.tilt：只给开口南 / 东南 / 西南三个姿势命名
def tiltOf (d : Int) : Option Int :=
  if d == 6 then some 0 else if d == 7 then some 1 else if d == 5 then some (-1) else none

-- bar_rule 的闭式：自最近一次归零以来走了 k 步，色 4 格数 = 64 - round(64k/100)（半数向上）
def barAt (k : Int) : Int := 64 - (64 * k + 50) / 100

-- ---------------- 位掩码（格 (r,c) 占第 r*64+c 位；Nat 上做位运算） ----------------
def M (x : Int) : Nat := x.toNat
def bitOf (r c : Int) : Nat := (r * 64 + c).toNat
-- 计数与质心用 SWAR：4096 位按行切成 64 个 64 位「格道」，一次大数运算同时处理 64 行。
-- 逐位循环（带累加器）在内核里会把未求值的累加式越堆越深，实测 consistent 吃到 10 GB 以上被杀。
def W64 : Nat := (1 <<< 64) - 1
def rep64 (n : Nat) : Nat := ((1 <<< (64 * n)) - 1) / W64          -- 每 64 位放一个 1、共 n 个
def lanes (w : Nat) : Nat := rep64 64 * w                            -- 64 位字 w 复制到 64 个格道
-- 每个格道（= 每一行）里 1 的个数，落在该格道的低字节
def rowCnt (x : Nat) : Nat :=
  let x1 := x - ((x >>> 1) &&& lanes (W64 / 3))
  let x2 := (x1 &&& lanes (W64 / 5)) + ((x1 >>> 2) &&& lanes (W64 / 5))
  let x3 := (x2 + (x2 >>> 4)) &&& lanes (W64 / 17)
  let x4 := x3 + (x3 >>> 8)
  let x5 := x4 + (x4 >>> 16)
  let x6 := x5 + (x5 >>> 32)
  x6 &&& lanes 255
-- 64 个格道的值加总（乘以全 1 格道模板后取第 63 格道）
def laneSum (y : Nat) : Nat := ((y * rep64 64) >>> (64 * 63)) &&& W64
def popN (x : Nat) : Nat := laneSum (rowCnt x)
def pop (n : Nat) : Int := Int.ofNat (popN n)
def minus (a b : Nat) : Nat := a ^^^ (a &&& b)
def within (a b : Nat) : Bool := a &&& b == a

-- 一行里的一段列 [a, b] -> 掩码（一次移位）
def rowRun (r a b : Int) : Nat := if a ≤ b then ((1 <<< (b - a + 1).toNat) - 1) <<< bitOf r a else 0
-- 同一行或同一列上的一段格（端点顺序任意）-> 掩码，常数次大数运算，不逐格
def segMask (r0 c0 r1 c1 : Int) : Nat :=
  let ra := min r0 r1
  let rb := max r0 r1
  let ca := min c0 c1
  let cb := max c0 c1
  if ra == rb then rowRun ra ca cb else rep64 (rb - ra + 1).toNat <<< bitOf ra ca

-- floor(x / 2)，不依赖 Int 除法的取整约定
def fdiv2 (x : Int) : Int := if 0 ≤ x then x / 2 else -((1 - x) / 2)

-- ---------------- 容器（vessel bbox 在状态里）与轨道中心 ----------------
def hr2 (s : State) : Int := s.vessel_0 + s.vessel_1      -- 容器中心行 x2
def hc2 (s : State) : Int := s.vessel_2 + s.vessel_3      -- 容器中心列 x2

def vrect (s : State) : Nat :=
  (rep64 (s.vessel_1 - s.vessel_0 + 1).toNat * ((1 <<< (s.vessel_3 - s.vessel_2 + 1).toNat) - 1))
    <<< bitOf s.vessel_0 s.vessel_2
def vesIn (s : State) (m : Int) : Nat := M m &&& vrect s

-- a5_paints_cup_side（_lib.side_halfplane）：法向 u = DIR_VEC[(d+4)%8]、过 bbox 中心，含界线：
--   u.1*(2i-(h-1)) + u.2*(2j-(h-1)) >= 0（i, j 为容器内相对行列）。逐行解出 j 的区间，一行一次移位。
def halfPlane (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat :=
  let h := s.vessel_1 - s.vessel_0 + 1
  let w := s.vessel_3 - s.vessel_2 + 1
  let u := dirVec ((d + 4) % 8)
  (List.range h.toNat).foldl (fun m i =>
    let A := u.1 * (2 * Int.ofNat i - (h - 1))
    let lo : Int := if u.2 == 1 then max 0 (-(fdiv2 (A - (h - 1)))) else 0
    let hi : Int := if u.2 == -1 then min (w - 1) (fdiv2 (h - 1 + A))
                    else if u.2 == 0 && A < 0 then -1 else w - 1
    m ||| rowRun (s.vessel_0 + Int.ofNat i) (s.vessel_2 + lo) (s.vessel_2 + hi)) 0

-- a5_fill_halfplane 的三档（它自己的 _halfplane：tilt 0 为行 <= (top+bot)/2；-1 为 j >= i 的右上三角；
-- +1 为 j <= (w-1)-i 的左上三角）
def halfPlane3 (s : State) (tl : Int) : Nat :=
  let h := s.vessel_1 - s.vessel_0 + 1
  let w := s.vessel_3 - s.vessel_2 + 1
  let mid := (s.vessel_0 + s.vessel_1) / 2
  (List.range h.toNat).foldl (fun m i =>
    let r := s.vessel_0 + Int.ofNat i
    let lo : Int := if tl == -1 then Int.ofNat i else 0
    let hi : Int := if tl == 0 then (if r ≤ mid then w - 1 else -1)
                    else if tl == -1 then w - 1 else w - 1 - Int.ofNat i
    m ||| rowRun r (s.vessel_2 + lo) (s.vessel_2 + hi)) 0

-- 容器里被涂成颜料色 pc 的格 m：fill_m 只记色 15，所以 pc=15 时并进去，否则从色 15 里减掉
def paintInto (v m : Nat) (pc : Int) : Nat := if pc == 15 then v ||| m else minus v m

-- ---------------- 大杯的八个姿势（rot8_apply） ----------------
-- 姿势 6（开口南）= _lib.start_cup()，即 _shape(START_POSE)；姿势 7 = rot8_apply 从姿势 6 沿边 6 转一格
-- （枢轴 hub+(1.5,-3.5)）的重画结果。两张都是 _lib 几何算出来的（生成脚本 _scratch/cd82-20260912/consts.py），
-- 不是逐帧抄的；但 START_POSE 与 EDGE_OFFSET 本身是早期对帧拟合出的游戏内容常数，栅格化（_shape）在 Python 里做。rot8_apply 的「相隔 90 度的两条边互为绕 hub 的 90 度旋转」让其余六个姿势 = 这两张绕容器中心整格转。
def pose6InkRuns : List (Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(24,25,38), (25,25,25), (25,38,38), (26,25,25), (26,38,38), (27,25,25), (27,38,38), (28,25,25), (28,38,38),
   (29,25,25), (29,38,38), (30,25,25), (30,38,38), (31,25,25), (31,38,38), (32,25,25), (32,38,38)]
def pose6LiqRuns : List (Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(25,26,37), (26,26,37), (27,26,37), (28,26,37), (29,26,37), (30,26,37), (31,26,37)]
def pose7InkRuns : List (Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(21,24,24), (22,23,25), (23,22,23), (23,25,26), (24,21,22), (24,26,27), (25,20,21), (25,27,28), (26,19,20),
   (26,28,29), (27,18,19), (27,29,30), (28,17,18), (29,16,17), (30,15,16), (31,14,15), (32,15,16), (33,16,17),
   (34,17,18), (35,18,19), (36,19,20), (37,20,20)]
def pose7LiqRuns : List (Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(23,24,24), (24,23,25), (25,22,26), (26,21,27), (27,20,28), (28,19,28), (29,18,27), (30,17,26), (31,16,25),
   (32,17,24), (33,18,23), (34,19,22), (35,20,21)]

-- 双倍相对坐标下的 90 度转：逆时针 (dr,dc)->(-dc,dr)（开口下标 +2），顺时针 (dr,dc)->(dc,-dr)
def ccwRel : Nat → Int × Int → Int × Int
  | 0, p => p
  | n + 1, p => ccwRel n (-p.2, p.1)
def cwRel : Nat → Int × Int → Int × Int
  | 0, p => p
  | n + 1, p => cwRel n (p.2, -p.1)

-- 格 (r,c) 绕容器中心逆时针转 q 个直角（中心在半格上，双倍坐标下精确）
def rotPt (s : State) (q : Nat) (r c : Int) : Int × Int :=
  let x := ccwRel q (2 * r - hr2 s, 2 * c - hc2 s)
  ((hr2 s + x.1) / 2, (hc2 s + x.2) / 2)

-- 行段表整体绕容器中心转 q 个直角：每段两个端点转过去，仍是一段（横段转成竖段），一段一次运算
def runsRot (s : State) (q : Nat) (rs : List (Int × Int × Int)) : Nat :=
  rs.foldl (fun m t =>
    let a := rotPt s q t.1 t.2.1
    let b := rotPt s q t.1 t.2.2
    m ||| segMask a.1 a.2 b.1 b.2) 0

def poseInk (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat :=
  if d % 2 == 0 then runsRot s ((d - 6) % 8 / 2).toNat pose6InkRuns
  else runsRot s ((d - 7) % 8 / 2).toNat pose7InkRuns
def poseLiq (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat :=
  if d % 2 == 0 then runsRot s ((d - 6) % 8 / 2).toNat pose6LiqRuns
  else runsRot s ((d - 7) % 8 / 2).toNat pose7LiqRuns

-- ---------------- 第二只小杯（secondcup_returns_where 的 HOPPER_REL，开口南时量的双倍相对偏移） ----------------
-- HOPPER_REL 的 28 格按角色拆成横 / 竖段（双倍相对坐标，相邻格差 2）：(起行, 起列, 止行, 止列)
def rimSegs : List (Int × Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(-41,-3,-41,3), (-39,-5,-39,-3), (-39,3,-39,5), (-37,-5,-33,-5), (-37,5,-33,5)]     -- 14 格描边
def neckSegs : List (Int × Int × Int × Int) := [(-39,-1,-39,1)]                         -- 2 格颈
def cavSegs : List (Int × Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(-37,-3,-37,3), (-35,-3,-35,3), (-33,-3,-33,3)]                                       -- 12 格腔
-- a6_spout_rim_stamps 的落点带（_lib.after_stamp 在开口南时：容器近边往里 3 行 x 腔的 4 列）
def stampSegs : List (Int × Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(-9,-3,-9,3), (-7,-3,-7,3), (-5,-3,-5,3)]

-- 摊在容器中心上，再绕容器中心顺时针转 (6 - d)/2 个直角
def placeSegs (s : State) (d : Int) (sg : List (Int × Int × Int × Int)) : Nat :=
  let q := ((6 - d) % 8 / 2).toNat
  sg.foldl (fun m t =>
    let a := cwRel q (t.1, t.2.1)
    let b := cwRel q (t.2.2.1, t.2.2.2)
    m ||| segMask ((hr2 s + a.1) / 2) ((hc2 s + a.2) / 2) ((hr2 s + b.1) / 2) ((hc2 s + b.2) / 2)) 0

-- 这一关有没有小杯：横带色环数 >= 7（secondcup_returns_where 第四句）；只在正向工位露面（secondcup_only_cardinal）
def spoutHere (s : State) (d : Int) : Bool := 7 ≤ s.swatches_n && d % 2 == 0
def rimM (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat := if spoutHere s d then placeSegs s d rimSegs else 0
def neckM (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat := if spoutHere s d then placeSegs s d neckSegs else 0
def cavM (s : State) (d : Int) : Nat := if spoutHere s d then placeSegs s d cavSegs else 0

def inGrid64 (r c : Int) : Bool := 0 ≤ r && 0 ≤ c && c < 64
def onRim (s : State) (r c : Int) : Bool := inGrid64 r c && Nat.testBit (rimM s s.open_dir) (bitOf r c)

-- ---------------- 色板（ring_code：每环 (上边行, 左列, 芯色)，空槽 -1） ----------------
def rings (s : State) : List (Int × Int × Int) :=
  [(s.ring_code_0, s.ring_code_1, s.ring_code_2), (s.ring_code_3, s.ring_code_4, s.ring_code_5),
   (s.ring_code_6, s.ring_code_7, s.ring_code_8), (s.ring_code_9, s.ring_code_10, s.ring_code_11),
   (s.ring_code_12, s.ring_code_13, s.ring_code_14), (s.ring_code_15, s.ring_code_16, s.ring_code_17),
   (s.ring_code_18, s.ring_code_19, s.ring_code_20)].filter (fun q => 0 ≤ q.2.1)

-- 点在哪个环的 5x5 里（含色 4 边框）：返回 (左列, 芯色)
def ringHit (s : State) (r c : Int) : Option (Int × Int) :=
  ((rings s).find? (fun q => q.1 ≤ r && r ≤ q.1 + 4 && q.2.1 ≤ c && c ≤ q.2.1 + 4)).map (fun q => (q.2.1, q.2.2))

-- 芯色为 col 的那些环的左列
def ringLeftsOf (s : State) (col : Int) : List Int := ((rings s).filter (fun q => q.2.2 == col)).map (fun q => q.2.1)

-- ---------------- 容器逐格颜色（vessel_cells 读数，生成器按颜色拆成掩码） ----------------
def vesCols : List Int := [0, 8, 9, 11, 12, 14, 15]
def vesOf (s : State) (col : Int) : Nat :=
  if col == 0 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c0 else if col == 8 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c8
  else if col == 9 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c9 else if col == 11 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c11
  else if col == 12 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c12 else if col == 14 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c14
  else if col == 15 then M s.vessel_cells_m_c15 else 0
-- 把区域 m 涂成颜料色 pc 之后，颜色 col 那张掩码
def paintCol (s : State) (m : Nat) (pc col : Int) : Nat :=
  if col == pc then vesOf s col ||| m else minus (vesOf s col) m
-- t 的容器逐色等于「s 的容器把 m 涂成 pc」
def vesPainted (s t : State) (m : Nat) (pc : Int) : Bool :=
  vesCols.all (fun col => vesOf t col == paintCol s m pc col)

def panelOf (s : State) (col : Int) : Nat :=
  if col == 0 then M s.panel_cells_m_c0 else if col == 8 then M s.panel_cells_m_c8
  else if col == 9 then M s.panel_cells_m_c9 else if col == 11 then M s.panel_cells_m_c11
  else if col == 12 then M s.panel_cells_m_c12 else if col == 14 then M s.panel_cells_m_c14
  else if col == 15 then M s.panel_cells_m_c15 else 0
-- 面板目标图 _lib.PANEL_TGT = 行 3..12 x 列 3..12
-- （主会话补注，审计要求）这是从观测反推的位置常数：每关面板图案落在 4/5 色边框内的这块区域，规则里不从像素定位它；
-- 每关的图案内容则是从面板像素 panel_cells 逐关读的（跨关会变——概念原文「面板整局一格没变」不准确）。
def picRect : Nat := (rep64 10 * ((1 <<< 10) - 1)) <<< bitOf 3 3
def squareVessel (s : State) : Bool :=
  s.vessel_1 - s.vessel_0 == s.vessel_3 - s.vessel_2 && s.vessel_1 - s.vessel_0 == 9
-- levelup_needs_target_pic：容器把 m 涂成 pc 之后，逐格等于面板目标图（容器 (r,c) 对面板 (r-上+3, c-左+3)）。
-- 逐色比：容器的 100 格全落在 vesCols 里（vessel_frozen：实心），每色对上就是逐格相等。
def hitsPic (s : State) (m : Nat) (pc : Int) : Bool :=
  vesCols.all (fun col => paintCol s m pc col >>> bitOf (s.vessel_0 - 3) (s.vessel_2 - 3) == panelOf s col &&& picRect)

-- 这一步涂进容器的区域（涂成当前颜料色）：A5 = a5_paints_cup_side 的半平面；点小杯描边 = 盖章带；其余为空
def paintRegion (s : State) (a : Act) : Nat :=
  match a with
  | Act.a5 => halfPlane s s.open_dir
  | Act.a6 x y =>
    if (ringHit s (Int.ofNat y) (Int.ofNat x)).isNone && onRim s (Int.ofNat y) (Int.ofNat x)
    then placeSegs s s.open_dir stampSegs else 0
  | _ => 0

-- ---------------- 由 (开口, 颜料, 钟, 涂色区域, 下划线) 算出全部可比字段 ----------------
-- pm：这一步用颜料 pc 涂进容器的区域（0 = 容器不动）
-- （主会话补注，审计要求）after_pour_* 是 halfPlane(open_dir, paint_color, 容器) 的函数，是冗余量：
-- 它在 keyEq 里占 9 个字段，但这些字段的匹配不构成独立验证；涂色的实证在 vessel_cells_m_* 的逐色比对。
def mkNext (s : State) (d pc k : Int) (pm : Nat) (u1 u2 : Int) : State :=
  let ves15 := paintCol s pm pc 15
  let ink := poseInk s d
  let liq := poseLiq s d
  let rim := rimM s d
  let neck := neckM s d
  let cav := cavM s d
  let sp := rim ||| neck ||| cav
  let cupLiq := liq ||| cav ||| (if pc == 0 then neck else 0)
  let inkAll := ink ||| rim
  let obj := ink ||| liq
  let pcells := vrect s ||| liq ||| cav ||| neck
  let fill := ves15 ||| (if pc == 15 then liq ||| cav else 0)
  let hp := halfPlane s d
  { s with
    open_dir := d, paint_color := pc, tilt := tiltOf d, h_k := k, bar := barAt k,
    underline_1 := u1, underline_2 := u2,
    cup_ink_m := Int.ofNat ink, cup_ink_n := pop ink,
    liquid_m := Int.ofNat liq, liquid_n := pop liq,
    cup_liquid_m := Int.ofNat cupLiq, cup_liquid_n := pop cupLiq,
    ink_m := Int.ofNat inkAll, ink_n := pop inkAll,
    obj_m := Int.ofNat obj, obj_n := pop obj,
    spout_m := Int.ofNat sp, spout_n := pop sp,
    paint_cells_m := Int.ofNat pcells, paint_cells_n := pop pcells,
    fill_m := Int.ofNat fill, fill_n := pop fill,
    vessel_cells_m_c0 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 0), vessel_cells_m_c8 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 8),
    vessel_cells_m_c9 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 9), vessel_cells_m_c11 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 11),
    vessel_cells_m_c12 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 12), vessel_cells_m_c14 := Int.ofNat (paintCol s pm pc 14),
    vessel_cells_m_c15 := Int.ofNat ves15,
    cup_blobs_n := if sp == 0 then 1 else 2,
    after_pour_m := Int.ofNat hp, after_pour_n := pop hp,
    after_pour_m_c0 := if pc == 0 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c8 := if pc == 8 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c9 := if pc == 9 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c11 := if pc == 11 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c12 := if pc == 12 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c14 := if pc == 14 then Int.ofNat hp else 0,
    after_pour_m_c15 := if pc == 15 then Int.ofNat hp else 0 }

-- 关卡内一步：
--   A1..A4  orbit_turn_rule 给新开口（死键不转），rot8_apply 给新姿势，小杯随工位出没；
--   A5      a5_paints_cup_side 的半平面涂进容器；
--   A6      点环 -> a6_swatch_sets_paint 换颜料 + underline_relocates_on_recolor 挪下划线；
--           点小杯描边 -> a6_spout_rim_stamps 盖章；其余 -> a6_dead_outside_swatches 死键；
--   每一步 bar_rule 的钟 h_k 加一。
--   levelup_needs_target_pic 判定这一步会过关（涂完的容器 == 面板目标图）时返回 none：
--   过关后新关的面板、色板是游戏内容，只能查 worldRows；所以路线上每个关卡内步都由 Lean 判定「不过关」。
--   RESET 不在这里算（路线上没有 RESET）。
def stepRules (s : State) (a : Act) : Option State :=
  let k := s.h_k + 1
  let d := s.open_dir
  let pc := s.paint_color
  let pm := paintRegion s a
  if hitsPic s pm pc then none else
  match a with
  | Act.a5 => some (mkNext s d pc k pm s.underline_1 s.underline_2)
  | Act.a6 x y =>
    match ringHit s (Int.ofNat y) (Int.ofNat x) with
    | some rc => some (mkNext s d rc.2 k 0 rc.1 (rc.1 + 4))
    | none => some (mkNext s d pc k pm s.underline_1 s.underline_2)
  | Act.reset => none
  | Act.other => none
  | _ =>
    match turnOf s a with
    | some nd => some (mkNext s nd pc k 0 s.underline_1 s.underline_2)
    | none => some (mkNext s d pc k 0 s.underline_1 s.underline_2)

-- ---------------- 规则用的判据小件 ----------------
def sameLv (s t : State) : Bool := s.level == t.level

-- 两张掩码只在容器矩形里不同
def sameOutside (s : State) (a b : Int) : Bool := within (M a ^^^ M b) (vrect s)

def panelEq (s t : State) : Bool :=
  s.panel_cells_m == t.panel_cells_m && s.panel_cells_m_c0 == t.panel_cells_m_c0
  && s.panel_cells_m_c4 == t.panel_cells_m_c4 && s.panel_cells_m_c5 == t.panel_cells_m_c5
  && s.panel_cells_m_c8 == t.panel_cells_m_c8 && s.panel_cells_m_c9 == t.panel_cells_m_c9
  && s.panel_cells_m_c11 == t.panel_cells_m_c11 && s.panel_cells_m_c12 == t.panel_cells_m_c12
  && s.panel_cells_m_c14 == t.panel_cells_m_c14 && s.panel_cells_m_c15 == t.panel_cells_m_c15

def ringEq (s t : State) : Bool :=
  rings s == rings t && s.swatches_n == t.swatches_n && s.paints_n == t.paints_n

-- 舞台上「所有杯」占的格：描边（含小杯）+ 大杯 + 小杯 + 各杯里的颜料
def cupsOf (s : State) : Nat := M s.ink_m ||| M s.obj_m ||| M s.spout_m ||| M s.cup_liquid_m

-- 除预算条（bar 与它的钟）之外，全部可比字段不变
def stillExceptBar (s t : State) : Bool := keyEq { t with bar := s.bar, h_k := s.h_k } s

-- 下划线恰好在「芯色 == 颜料色」的唯一那个环下
def underlineOk (g : State) : Bool :=
  match ringLeftsOf g g.paint_color with
  | [l] => g.underline_1 == l && g.underline_2 == l + 4
  | _ => false

-- 杯质心要的 (行和, 列和, 格数)：
--   行和 = Σ 行号 x 该行格数：各行格数（rowCnt）乘以「第 k 格道放 k」的模板，第 63 格道得 Σ (63 - 行号) x 格数；
--   列和 = Σ_j 2^j x #{格 : 列号第 j 位为 1}（按列号二进制位拆开数）。
def rampLanes : Nat := (List.range 64).foldl (fun a k => a ||| (k <<< (64 * k))) 0
def colBit (j : Nat) : Nat :=      -- 64 位字里「下标第 j 位为 1」的那些位
  if j == 0 then 2 * (W64 / 3) else if j == 1 then 4 * (W64 / 5) else if j == 2 then 16 * (W64 / 17)
  else if j == 3 then (W64 / 257) <<< 8 else if j == 4 then (W64 / 65537) <<< 16 else (W64 / ((1 <<< 32) + 1)) <<< 32
def cen (m : Int) : Int × Int × Int :=
  let x := M m
  let y := rowCnt x
  let n := laneSum y
  let rs := 63 * n - (((y * rampLanes) >>> (64 * 63)) &&& W64)
  let cs := (List.range 6).foldl (fun a j => a + (1 <<< j) * popN (x &&& lanes (colBit j))) 0
  (Int.ofNat rs, Int.ofNat cs, Int.ofNat n)

-- 与向量夹角最小的八方向（_lib.dir8）：比较带符号的 (dot/|u|)^2，两边乘 2 全是整数
def sgnSq (x : Int) : Int := if x < 0 then -(x * x) else x * x
def dirScore (v : Int × Int) (d : Int) : Int :=
  let u := dirVec d
  let dot := v.1 * u.1 + v.2 * u.2
  if u.1 != 0 && u.2 != 0 then sgnSq dot else 2 * sgnSq dot
def best8 (v : Int × Int) : Int :=
  [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].foldl (fun b i => if dirScore v b < dirScore v i then i else b) 0

-- open_faces_vessel：从杯质心指向容器中心的八方向 == 开口方向（乘 2n 去分母）
def facesHub (g : State) : Bool :=
  let q := cen g.obj_m
  q.2.2 == 0 || best8 (q.2.2 * hr2 g - 2 * q.1, q.2.2 * hc2 g - 2 * q.2.1) == g.open_dir

-- keys_are_screen_dirs：质心位移（乘 n1*n2 去分母）的主分量方向；两分量等长返回 (0,0)
def mainDir (s t : State) : Int × Int :=
  let p := cen s.obj_m
  let q := cen t.obj_m
  let dr := q.1 * p.2.2 - p.1 * q.2.2
  let dc := q.2.1 * p.2.2 - p.2.1 * q.2.2
  let adr := if dr < 0 then -dr else dr
  let adc := if dc < 0 then -dc else dc
  if adr < adc then (0, if 0 < dc then 1 else -1)
  else if adc < adr then (if 0 < dr then 1 else -1, 0)
  else (0, 0)

-- ---------------- 30 条在册概念 ----------------
-- 没写进来的一条：
--   budget_zero_gameover：它断言的是引擎给的 state（NOT_FINISHED -> GAME_OVER），State 里没有这个量，
--     帧上也读不出（概念自己就说「不从像素读终局」）。写进来只能是空话，所以不写。
-- 其余每条的 guard 取 assert_ 不返回 NA 的那部分动作；换关（sameLv 为假）时概念返回 NA，effect 相应放行。
def rules : List Rule := [
  { name := "a12_only_cup",
    guard := fun _ a => match a with | Act.a1 => true | Act.a2 => true | _ => false,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (within (M s.paint_cells_m ^^^ M t.paint_cells_m) (cupsOf s ||| cupsOf t)
        && within (M s.ink_m ^^^ M t.ink_m) (cupsOf s ||| cupsOf t)
        && within (M s.fill_m ^^^ M t.fill_m) (cupsOf s ||| cupsOf t)
        && vesPainted s t 0 s.paint_color
        && panelEq s t && ringEq s t && t.underline_1 == s.underline_1 && t.underline_2 == s.underline_2) },

  { name := "a5_fill_halfplane",
    guard := fun s a => isA5 a && s.tilt.isSome,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match s.tilt with
       | some tl => vesPainted s t (halfPlane3 s tl) s.paint_color
       | none => false) },

  { name := "a5_paints_cup_side",
    guard := fun _ a => isA5 a,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (squareVessel s && vesPainted s t (halfPlane s s.open_dir) s.paint_color) },

  { name := "a5_pours_into_vessel",
    guard := fun _ a => isA5 a,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (sameOutside s s.ink_m t.ink_m && sameOutside s s.cup_ink_m t.cup_ink_m
        && sameOutside s s.liquid_m t.liquid_m && sameOutside s s.cup_liquid_m t.cup_liquid_m
        && sameOutside s s.spout_m t.spout_m && sameOutside s s.obj_m t.obj_m
        && sameOutside s s.paint_cells_m t.paint_cells_m && sameOutside s s.fill_m t.fill_m
        -- 容器里变了的格一律变成当前颜料色：除颜料色之外，每种颜色的格只减不增
        && vesCols.all (fun col => col == s.paint_color || within (vesOf t col) (vesOf s col))) },

  { name := "a6_dead_outside_swatches",
    guard := fun s a => match clickOf a with
      | some p => (ringHit s p.1 p.2).isNone && !onRim s p.1 p.2
      | none => false,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || stillExceptBar s t },

  { name := "a6_spout_rim_stamps",
    guard := fun s a => match clickOf a with
      | some p => onRim s p.1 p.2
      | none => false,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (vesPainted s t (placeSegs s s.open_dir stampSegs) s.paint_color && t.spout_m == s.spout_m) },

  { name := "a6_swatch_sets_paint",
    guard := fun s a => match clickOf a with
      | some p => (ringHit s p.1 p.2).isSome
      | none => false,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match clickOf a with
       | some p =>
         (match ringHit s p.1 p.2 with
          | some rc =>
            t.paint_color == rc.2 && t.liquid_m == s.liquid_m && t.ink_m == s.ink_m && t.spout_m == s.spout_m
              && vesPainted s t 0 s.paint_color
              && minus (M t.fill_m) (vrect s) == (if rc.2 == 15 then M s.cup_liquid_m else 0)
          | none => false)
       | none => false) },

  { name := "bar_prefix_shrink",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (s.bar_is_prefix && t.bar_is_prefix
        && (if isReset a then t.bar == 64 else (s.bar - t.bar == 0 || s.bar - t.bar == 1))) },

  { name := "bar_rule",
    guard := fun _ a => !isReset a,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (t.h_k == s.h_k + 1 && t.bar == barAt (s.h_k + 1)) },

  { name := "cup_frozen_within_tilt",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || s.open_dir != t.open_dir
      || (t.ink_m == s.ink_m && t.liquid_m == s.liquid_m) },

  { name := "key_dead_by_open_angle",
    guard := fun _ a => isKey a,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match turnOf s a with
       | none => stillExceptBar s t
       | some _ => t.obj_m != s.obj_m) },

  { name := "keys_are_screen_dirs",
    guard := fun _ a => isKey a,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t || t.obj_m == s.obj_m ||
      (match keyDir a with
       | some k => mainDir s t == dirVec k
       | none => false) },

  { name := "levelup_resets_stage",
    guard := fun _ a => !isReset a,
    -- 过关且还有下一关（第 6 关 = 通关，state 变 WIN，舞台不重置）
    effect := fun s _ t => t.level != s.level + 1 || t.level == 6 ||
      (vesOf t 0 == vrect t && t.bar == 64
        && t.cup_ink_m == Int.ofNat (poseInk t 6) && t.liquid_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq t 6)) },

  { name := "levelup_needs_target_pic",
    guard := fun _ a => !isReset a,
    -- 过关 ⟺ 这一步之后的容器（A5 = 倾倒半平面、点小杯描边 = 盖章带、其余 = 原样，都涂当前颜料色）
    -- 逐格等于面板目标图。关卡号变小（不会在非 RESET 上发生）与非正方容器时概念返回 NA。
    effect := fun s a t => t.level < s.level || !squareVessel s ||
      ((s.level < t.level) == hitsPic s (paintRegion s a) s.paint_color) },

  { name := "levelup_sets_paint",
    guard := fun _ a => !isReset a,
    effect := fun s _ t => t.level != s.level + 1 || t.level == 6 || t.paint_color == 15 },

  { name := "open_faces_vessel",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (facesHub s && facesHub t) },

  { name := "orbit_turn_rule",
    guard := fun s a => (turnOf s a).isSome,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t || t.obj_m == s.obj_m || some t.open_dir == turnOf s a },

  { name := "panel_static",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (panelEq s t && ringEq s t) },

  { name := "reset_sets_state",
    guard := fun _ a => isReset a,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (t.tilt == some 0 && t.paint_color == 15 && t.bar == 64
        && t.cup_ink_m == Int.ofNat (poseInk s 6) && t.liquid_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq s 6)
        && vesOf t 0 == vrect t) },

  { name := "reset_wipes_stage",
    guard := fun _ a => isReset a,
    -- 不设换关守卫：不管哪一种重开，舞台只剩容器（整块色 0）+ 开局姿势的杯（液色 15），底行补满
    effect := fun s _ t =>
      t.bar == 64 && t.spout_m == 0 && vesOf t 0 == vrect s
        && t.ink_m == Int.ofNat (poseInk s 6) && t.liquid_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq s 6)
        && t.paint_cells_m == Int.ofNat (vrect s ||| poseLiq s 6)
        && t.fill_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq s 6) },

  { name := "rot8_apply",
    guard := fun _ a => isKey a,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match turnOf s a with
       | none => t.obj_m == s.obj_m && t.open_dir == s.open_dir
       | some nd => t.open_dir == nd && t.cup_ink_m == Int.ofNat (poseInk s nd)
                    && t.liquid_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq s nd)) },

  { name := "rot_apply",
    guard := fun s a => (match a with | Act.a3 => true | Act.a4 => true | _ => false)
      && s.tilt.isSome && (match turnOf s a with | some nd => (tiltOf nd).isSome | none => false),
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match turnOf s a with
       | some nd => t.cup_ink_m == Int.ofNat (poseInk s nd) && t.liquid_m == Int.ofNat (poseLiq s nd)
       | none => false) },

  { name := "secondcup_at_level2_cardinals",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t =>
      (s.level != 2 || s.open_dir % 2 != 0 || s.spout_m != 0)
        && (t.level != 2 || t.open_dir % 2 != 0 || t.spout_m != 0) },

  { name := "secondcup_only_cardinal",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (s.spout_m == 0 && t.spout_m == 0) ||
      ((s.spout_m == 0 || s.open_dir % 2 == 0) && (t.spout_m == 0 || t.open_dir % 2 == 0)
        && (s.spout_m == 0 || t.spout_m == 0 || s.open_dir != t.open_dir || s.spout_m == t.spout_m)
        && (s.spout_m == 0 || t.spout_m != 0 || M s.spout_m &&& (M t.paint_cells_m ||| M t.ink_m) == 0)) },

  { name := "secondcup_returns_where",
    guard := fun s a => isKey a && s.open_dir % 2 == 1 && (turnOf s a).isSome,
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (match turnOf s a with
       | some nd =>
         let rim := placeSegs s nd rimSegs
         let neck := placeSegs s nd neckSegs
         let cav := placeSegs s nd cavSegs
         if 7 ≤ s.swatches_n then
           M t.spout_m == (rim ||| neck ||| cav) && within rim (M t.ink_m) && within cav (M t.cup_liquid_m)
             && within neck (M t.paint_cells_m) && (neck &&& M t.cup_liquid_m == 0 || t.paint_color == 0)
         else t.spout_m == 0 && (rim ||| neck ||| cav) &&& (M t.paint_cells_m ||| M t.ink_m) == 0
       | none => false) },

  { name := "target_panel_static",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (panelEq s t && t.panel_pic_n == s.panel_pic_n) },

  { name := "underline_marks_paint",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t || (underlineOk s && underlineOk t) },

  { name := "underline_relocates_on_recolor",
    guard := fun s a => isReset a || (match clickOf a with
      | some p => (ringHit s p.1 p.2).isSome
      | none => false),
    effect := fun s a t => !sameLv s t ||
      (let col : Int := if isReset a then 15 else
         (match clickOf a with
          | some p => (match ringHit s p.1 p.2 with | some rc => rc.2 | none => -1)
          | none => -1)
       match ringLeftsOf s col with
       | [l] => t.underline_1 == l && t.underline_2 == l + 4
       | _ => false) },

  { name := "vessel_frozen",
    guard := fun _ _ => true,
    effect := fun s _ t => !sameLv s t ||
      (t.vessel_0 == s.vessel_0 && t.vessel_1 == s.vessel_1 && t.vessel_2 == s.vessel_2 && t.vessel_3 == s.vessel_3
        && within (vrect t) (M t.paint_cells_m) && vrect t &&& M t.ink_m == 0) },

  { name := "win_no_repaint",
    guard := fun s a => s.level == 5 && !isReset a,
    -- 通关（第 5 关 -> 第 6 关，state 变 WIN）那一步：容器 bbox 与底行之外一格不动
    effect := fun s _ t => t.level != 6 ||
      (sameOutside s s.ink_m t.ink_m && sameOutside s s.liquid_m t.liquid_m
        && sameOutside s s.cup_liquid_m t.cup_liquid_m && sameOutside s s.spout_m t.spout_m
        && sameOutside s s.obj_m t.obj_m && sameOutside s s.paint_cells_m t.paint_cells_m
        && sameOutside s s.fill_m t.fill_m && panelEq s t && ringEq s t
        && t.underline_1 == s.underline_1 && t.underline_2 == s.underline_2
        && t.open_dir == s.open_dir && t.paint_color == s.paint_color) }
]

def isGoal (s : State) : Bool := s.level == 6

-- 先查换关表（worldRows 只有换关那几步），查不到交给 stepRules
def step (s : State) (a : Act) : Option State :=
  match worldRows.find? (fun w => keyEq w.s s && w.a == a) with
  | some w => some w.s'
  | none => stepRules s a
-- ==== 规则段 END ====

-- ==== 定理段 ====
-- 规则与全部观测一致（内核判定）。
theorem consistent : checkRules rules corpus = true := by decide +kernel

-- 从第 0 关开局逐步重算整条路线，每一步都在 keyEq 的全部变化字段上等于观测，末步到达 WIN。
theorem faithful : (faithfulRun step start routeObs).map isGoal = some true := by decide +kernel

判据 1 · 解析：绿，(b) = 0

$ python run_rules.py

  • parse 的良构检查是「把读出来的世界画回去，和帧 4096 格逐格比」（杯按工位光栅化、小杯按工位转 90 度、下划线 = 颜料色的第二种读法）。

  • 良构检查会不会响：10 种破坏帧全抛（run_rules.py --reverse「破坏」那 10 行）。第一版「容器挪一格」破坏太温柔没抛（挪出来恰好像合法容器），改真挪后抛——记在 record.txt。

判据 4 · 对象：绿（7 个字段全有见证或写明的欠账）

World = target / palette / vessel / pose / paint / spout / budget。python ledger/field_split.py ledger/games/cd82/objects …：5 个单独分得开，见证（runs/_wb-cd82/witness.py 核过「只差这一位」）：

字段 见证（帧号） 抹掉之后分不开的是
budget 0 / 1（seq 1 前后，64→63） 还剩几步、会不会终局
target 531 / 733（第 3 / 4 关开局） 涂成什么样才过关
vessel 1 / 101 这一步之后等不等于目标图
pose 1 / 851（工位 6 / 7） ACTION5 涂哪半边
paint 1 / 303（颜料 15 / 0） 倒出来的颜色

两个没有单独见证，写成欠账（不是没人读：消融 palette 90、spout 78）： - palette：语料里目标图决定色板。能分开的实验：同一张目标图配不同色板——Lean 钥匙一就是它的纸面版。 - spout：(目标图, 工位) 决定它；它是关属性 hasSpout 的帧上投影（斜向帧读不出）。能分开的实验：一关色环 < 7 却有小杯。

卡 03 真动手：删 vessel_box（恒定）、合 cup_cells→pose、underline→paint、spout_cells→spout；动手前后两份分离表在 analysis/field_split.txt。 卡 06 三个读数重取（分离表 / 逐条消融 / 源码点名）一处没动，record.txt 第 4 轮逐字段两栏。 像素接触：python analysis/touch_check.py → 契约缺 无，像素接触 0 处 / 11 条规则。
